### [1] [Layout-Aware Representation Learning for Open-Set ID Fraud Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05215)
*Jinxing Li,Nicholas Ren,Cathy Chang,Hongkai Pan,Daniel George*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向文档版面感知的表征学习方法，用于身份文档欺诈检测中的开放集欺诈发现。通过将DINOv3适配到文档领域，采用上下文感知的SimMIM微调和监督度量学习（复合损失函数），仅用美国ID训练即可在加拿大ID版面分类上达到99.83%的准确率。在20,448张加拿大ID数据集中，嵌入空间分析发现了276例自适应物理欺诈案例，其中222例未被现有检测器发现。该方法支持基于相似性的传播，从单个确认种子扩展到元数据图无法关联的相关案例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 身份文档欺诈检测不是一个静态的二分类问题。攻击者会自适应地修改模板和伪造流程，导致历史欺诈标签失效，且成功的伪造会以大规模协同攻击的方式反复出现。因此，需要从封闭集分类转向开放集欺诈发现。

Method: 将DINOv3适配到文档领域，通过上下文感知的SimMIM（带掩码的图像建模）进行微调，并结合监督度量学习，使用复合损失函数（鼓励类间可分性和类内紧凑性）。仅用美国ID进行训练。

Result: 在加拿大ID的版面分类上达到99.83%准确率；在20,448张加拿大ID数据集中，嵌入空间分析发现276例自适应物理欺诈案例，其中222例未被现有检测器发现。

Conclusion: 版面感知的文档嵌入为在分布偏移下发现新型和大规模欺诈提供了面向生产的基准，支持基于相似性的扩展从单个种子案例发现元数据图无法关联的相关案例。

Abstract: Identity-document fraud detection is not a stationary binary classification problem. Adaptive attackers modify templates and fabrication pipelines, making historical fraud labels stale, and successful forgeries recur at scale as coherent campaigns. We therefore study layout-aware representation learning for open-set fraud discovery rather than only closed-set classification. We adapt DINOv3 to the document domain via context-aware SimMIM fine-tuning and supervised metric learning with composite loss that encourages inter-class separability and intra-class compactness. The model is trained with U.S. IDs only. With a lightweight MLP and softmax classifier, the embedding achieves 99.83% layout classification accuracy on Canadian layouts. Moreover, on a dataset of 20,448 Canadian IDs, embedding-space analysis surfaces 276 adaptive physical-fraud cases, including 222 not surfaced by incumbent detectors. The embedding supports similarity-based expansion from a single confirmed seed to additional related cases not linked by conventional metadata graphs. The layout-aware document embeddings provide a production-aligned basis for discovering novel and campaign-scale fraud under distribution shift.

</details>


### [2] [Query2Uncertainty: Robust Uncertainty Quantification and Calibration for 3D Object Detection under Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05328)
*Till Beemelmanns,Alexey Nekrasov,Stefan Vilceanu,Jonas Steinhaus,Timo Woopen,Bastian Leibe,Lutz Eckstein*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种密度感知的校准方法（Query2Uncertainty），通过耦合后验校准器与DETR风格3D检测器中潜目标查询的特征密度，实现了在分布内和分布偏移场景下对分类和边界框回归不确定性的联合重校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的3D目标检测器在校准方面表现不佳，尤其是在分布偏移场景下；后验校准方法虽然在分布内测试中有效，但无法适应分布偏移的场景。

Method: 利用DETR风格3D检测器中的潜目标查询特征（具有紧凑、位置和类别感知的特性）进行密度估计，通过拟合密度估计器将后验校准器与特征密度耦合，联合重校准分类和边界框回归的不确定性。

Result: 在多视角相机和基于LiDAR的检测器上，该方法在分布内和分布偏移场景下均一致优于标准后验校准方法。

Conclusion: Query2Uncertainty通过利用查询特征密度实现了对分布偏移的自适应校准，提升了3D目标检测在安全关键应用中的可靠性。

Abstract: Reliable uncertainty estimation for 3D object detection is critical for deploying safe autonomous systems, yet modern detectors remain poorly calibrated, especially under distribution shifts. Although post-hoc calibration methods address this issue and provide improved calibration for in-distribution tests, they fail to adapt in distribution-shifted scenarios. In this work, we address this issue and introduce a density-aware calibration method that couples post-hoc calibrators with the feature density of latent object queries from DETR-style 3D object detectors. These queries form a compact, location and class-aware feature, ideal for density estimation, allowing our approach to adjust model confidences in distribution-shift scenarios. By fitting a density estimator on these query features, our approach jointly recalibrates both classification and bounding box regression uncertainties. On both a multi-view camera and LiDAR-based detector, our approach consistently outperforms standard post-hoc methods in both in-distribution and distribution-shifted scenarios. Code available https://tillbeemelmanns.github.io/query2uncertainty/ .

</details>


### [3] [ViTok-v2: Scaling Native Resolution Auto-Encoders to 5 Billion Parameters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05331)
*Philippe Hansen-Estruch,Jiahui Chen,Vivek Ramanujan,Orr Zohar,Yan Ping,Animesh Sinha,Markos Georgopoulos,Edgar Schoenfeld,Ji Hou,Felix Juefei-Xu,Sriram Vishwanath,Ali Thabet*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ViTok-v2 是一个扩展至 5B 参数、基于 2B 图像训练的视觉自编码器，通过 NaFlex 支持原生分辨率及宽高比、用 DINOv3 感知损失替代 LPIPS 和 GAN 损失，实现了跨分辨率/宽高比的稳定训练与最先进的重建性能，并通过联合缩放自编码器与生成器推进了重建-生成的帕累托前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 ViT 自编码器在训练分辨率外性能下降，且依赖对抗损失导致无法稳定缩放；压缩比 r 在重建与生成之间存在权衡，改善自编码器重建是获得更优帕累托前沿的关键。

Method: 1) 采用 NaFlex 实现原生分辨率支持，泛化至不同分辨率和宽高比；2) 引入 DINOv3 感知损失替代 LPIPS 和 GAN 损失，实现任意规模的稳定训练；3) 在约 2B 图像上训练，模型规模扩展至 5B 参数。

Result: ViTok-v2 在 256p 分辨率上匹配或超越最先进重建性能，在 512p 及以上分辨率全面优于所有基线；联合缩放自编码器与流匹配生成器推进了帕累托前沿。

Conclusion: 通过原生分辨率支持和稳定训练损失设计，ViTok-v2 成为目前最大规模且跨分辨率表现最优的图像自编码器，验证了缩放自编码器与生成器对帕累托权衡的积极作用。

Abstract: Vision Transformer (ViT) autoencoders have emerged as compelling tokenizers for images, offering improved reconstruction over convolutional tokenizers. However, existing ViT tokenizers cannot explore this landscape as performance degrades outside training resolutions, and reliance on adversarial losses prevents stable scaling. ViTok (Hansen-Estruch et al., 2025) found that the compression ratio r mediates a reconstruction-generation trade-off where lower r means better reconstructions but harder generations, so improving tokenizer reconstruction is key to more Pareto-optimal tokenizers. We introduce ViTok-v2, which addresses these limitations with native resolution support via NaFlex for generalization across resolutions and aspect ratios, and a novel DINOv3 perceptual loss that replaces both LPIPS and GAN objectives for stable training at any scale. ViTok-v2 is trained on about 2B images and scaled to 5B parameters, the largest image autoencoder to date. ViTok-v2 matches or exceeds state-of-the-art reconstruction at 256p and outperforms all baselines at 512p and above. In joint scaling experiments with flow matching generators, we show that scaling both the autoencoder and the generator advances the Pareto frontier of this trade-off.

</details>


### [4] [Open-SAT: LLM-Guided Query Embedding Refinement for Open-Vocabulary Object Retrieval in Satellite Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05344)
*Md Adnan Arefeen,Biplob Debnath,Ravi K. Rajendran,Murugan Sankaradas,Srimat T. Chakradhar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Open-SAT是一种无需额外训练的查询嵌入精炼算法，通过LLM在推理阶段优化文本嵌入，并结合无阈值检索机制，显著提升开放词汇场景下卫星图像检索的准确性，F1分数最高提升16.04%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卫星应用中用户查询通常是开放式的自然语言，超出预定义类别范围，现有VLM（如CLIP）即使经过微调也难以准确对齐这些查询与卫星图像内容。

Method: Open-SAT是一种免训练的查询嵌入精炼算法：1）使用VLM计算图像块的嵌入并存入向量数据库；2）在查询时利用LLM通过引入感兴趣对象及其周围环境的上下文信息来精炼文本嵌入；3）采用无阈值检索机制提升准确性和效率。

Result: 在三个公开基准数据集上，Open-SAT将F1分数最高提升16.04%，同时检索到的图像块数量与基线相当。

Conclusion: Open-SAT在开放词汇卫星图像检索中表现出有效性，通过LLM引导在不需额外训练或监督的情况下提升了检索性能。

Abstract: In satellite applications, user queries often take the form of open-ended natural language, extending beyond a fixed set of predefined categories. This open-vocabulary nature poses significant challenges for retrieving relevant image tiles, as the retrieval system must generalize to a wide range of unseen objects and concepts. While vision-language models (VLMs) such as CLIP are widely used for text-image retrieval, even fine-tuned variants often struggle to accurately align such queries with satellite imagery. To address this, we propose Open-SAT, a training-free query embedding refinement algorithm that operates at inference time to improve alignment between user queries and satellite image content. Open-SAT uses VLMs to compute embeddings for image tiles, which are stored in a vector database for efficient retrieval. At query time, it leverages Large Language Models (LLMs) to refine the text embeddings by incorporating contextual information about objects of interest and their surroundings. A threshold-free retrieval mechanism further enhances accuracy and efficiency. Experimental results in three public benchmarks demonstrate that Open-SAT improves the F1 score by up to 16.04%, while retrieving a comparable number of image tiles. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of Open-SAT in open-vocabulary satellite image retrieval, leveraging LLM guidance without the need for additional training or supervision.

</details>


### [5] [egenioussBench: A New Dataset for Geospatial Visual Localisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05351)
*Phillipp Fanta-Jende,Francesco Vultaggio,Alexander Kern,Yasmin Loeper,Markus Gerke*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了egenioussBench，一个基于城市级3D网格和CityGML LoD2模型的地理空间视觉定位基准，包含非共视图像测试集和带有姿态的验证集，支持跨视图和跨域的大规模视觉定位评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉定位基准多基于SfM重建，难以扩展到城市级大规模场景，且缺乏与地理空间参考数据（如3D网格和CityGML模型）的紧密结合，无法真实反映可部署地图资产的定位挑战。

Method: 利用城市级机载3D网格和CityGML LoD2模型作为参考数据；通过PPK和GCP/CP辅助平差获取厘米级精度真值；从2709张图像中通过渲染深度估计完整共视矩阵并选取最大独立集，得到42张非共视图像测试集和412张序列图像验证集；采用多误差阈值分箱度量与全局统计指标进行公平评估。

Result: 构建了包含42张非共视测试图像（真值保密）和412张序列验证图像（含姿态）的基准数据集，并提供了公共排行榜和评估代码。

Conclusion: 该基准通过精心设计暴露了真实的跨视图和跨域挑战，为推进大规模视觉定位提供了严格的、可扩展的评估路径。

Abstract: We present egenioussBench, a visual localisation benchmark built on geospatial reference data: a city-scale airborne 3D mesh and a CityGML LoD2 model. This pairing reflects deployable mapping assets and supports true scalability beyond traditional SfM-based approaches. The query data comprise smartphone images with centimetre-accurate, map-independent ground truth obtained via PPK and GCP/CP-aided adjustment. From 2,709 images, we derive a non-co-visible subset by estimating the full co-visibility matrix from rendered depth and selecting a maximum independent set; the released data include a test split of 42 non-co-visible images with withheld ground truth and a validation split of 412 sequential images with poses, e.g. for training of pose regressors and self-validation. The benchmark features a public leaderboard evaluated with binning metrics at multiple pose-error thresholds alongside global statistics (median, RMSE, outlier ratio), ensuring fair, like-for-like comparison across mesh- and LoD2-based methods. Together, these design choices expose realistic cross-view and cross-domain challenges while providing a rigorous, scalable path for advancing large-scale visual localisation. We make the evaluation code and data availeable at https://github.com/fratopa/egenioussBench and https://www.egeniouss.eu/

</details>


### [6] [LAMP: Localization Aware Multi-camera People Tracking in Metric 3D World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05390)
*Nan Yang,Julian Straub,Fan Zhang,Richard Newcombe,Jakob Engel,Lingni Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LAMP提出了一种"先提升后拟合"的两阶段框架，通过分离观察者与被观察者运动，利用已知的6DoF设备运动和标定信息，将多相机2D关键点转换到统一3D世界坐标系，再使用时空transformer直接拟合3D人体运动，在自我中心多相机场景中取得了最先进效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单目人体运动追踪方法在处理自我中心多相机头戴设备时面临严重挑战：剧烈的自身运动、部分可见性或遮挡、缺乏训练数据，且无法有效利用多视角、已标定和已定位的输入信息，导致在动态自我中心捕捉中表现脆弱易失败。

Method: 两阶段方法：1) 利用已知设备6DoF运动和相机标定，将时间窗口内所有相机的2D人体关键点提升到统一3D世界坐标系；2) 使用端到端训练的时空transformer，直接从这个3D射线点云中拟合3D人体运动。这种"先提升后拟合"策略使得模型能在世界空间中学习和利用自然人体运动先验。

Result: 在单目基准测试上达到最先进水平，并在自我中心多相机场景中显著优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过早期解耦观察者与被观察者运动，LAMP提供了一种优雅的框架来灵活整合来自多个时间异步、部分观察且移动的相机信息，有效解决了自我中心多相机人体追踪的核心难题。

Abstract: Tracking 3D human motion from egocentric multi-camera headset is challenged by severe egomotion, partial visibility or occlusions and lack of training data. Existing methods designed for monocular video often require static or slowly-moving cameras and cannot efficiently leverage multi-view, calibrated and localized input. This makes them brittle and prone to fail on dynamic egocentric captures. We propose LAMP (Localization Aware Multi-camera People Tracking): a novel, simple framework to solve this via early disentanglement of observer and target motion. LAMP introduces a two-step process. First, we leverage the known device 6 DoF motion and calibration to convert detected 2D body keypoints from all cameras over a temporal window into a unified 3D world reference frame. Second, an end-to-end-trained spatio-temporal transformer fits 3D human motion directly to this 3D ray cloud. This "lift-then-fit" approach allows LAMP to learn and leverage a natural human motion prior in the world-space, as well as providing an elegant framework to flexibly incorporate information from multiple temporally asynchronous, partially observing and moving cameras. LAMP achieves state-of-the-art results on monocular benchmarks, while significantly outperforming baselines for our targeted egocentric setting.

</details>


### [7] [EchoXFlow: A Beamspace Echocardiography Dataset for Cardiac Motion, Flow, and Function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05447)
*Elias Stenhede,Joanna Sulkowska,Eivind Bjørkan Orstad,Henrik Schirmer,Arian Ranjbar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EchoXFlow是一个临床超声心动图数据集，包含37125条记录和666次临床检查，保留了原始采集几何信息（时序、空间、模态关系），提供分离的模态流（1D/2D/3D数据及多普勒模态）和同步ECG，支持跨模态、采集感知的学习任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有公开数据集存在两个关键局限：一是通常缺失多普勒信息，或仅以RGB叠加方式融合；二是数据经过有损的厂商显示处理后发布，丢失了原始采集几何信息。这限制了跨模态学习以及基于物理特性的超声心动图学习研究。

Method: EchoXFlow从666次常规临床检查中收集37125条记录，保留原始采集的时序、几何和模态关系。每条记录以分离的模态流形式保留：时间分辨的1D、2D、3D数据以及多种多普勒模态，并配有同步ECG。临床标注涵盖从指南推荐测量到密集2D心肌轮廓和3D左心室心内膜网格。

Result: 构建了一个包含37125条记录、666次检查的超声心动图数据集，并提供开源工具支持。数据集保留了原始采集几何和模态分离特性，能够支持传统扫描转换视频无法实现的跨模态、采集感知学习任务。

Conclusion: EchoXFlow填补了现有数据集在原始采集几何和跨模态学习方面的空白，为4D视觉和基于物理的多模态学习提供了测试平台，能够支持传统扫描转换视频无法实现的学习任务。

Abstract: We introduce EchoXFlow, a clinical echocardiography dataset for learning from ultrasound in its native acquisition geometry rather than from scan-converted Cartesian videos. Existing public datasets offer limited opportunities to study cross-modal relationships between cardiac anatomy, myocardial motion, and blood flow, as Doppler is typically absent or fused as RGB overlays, and acquisitions are released after lossy vendor display processing. EchoXFlow comprises 37125 recordings from 666 routine-care examinations, preserving the timing, geometry, and modality relationships needed for physically grounded echo learning. Each recording is retained as separable modality-specific streams: temporally resolved 1D, 2D, and 3D data alongside multiple Doppler modalities, paired with a synchronized ECG. Clinical annotations span guideline-based measurements to dense 2D myocardial contours and 3D left-ventricular endocardial meshes. With its associated open-source tooling, EchoXFlow enables cross-modal, acquisition-aware learning tasks that cannot be formulated from conventional scan-converted videos alone, and serves as a testbed for 4D vision and physically grounded multi-modal learning more broadly.

</details>


### [8] [The First Controllable Bokeh Rendering Challenge at NTIRE 2026](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05510)
*Tim Seizinger,Florin-Alexandru Vasluianu,Jeffrey Chen,Zhuyun Zhou,Zongwei Wu,Radu Timofte,Dafeng Zhang,Yipeng Lin,Qi Yan,Junhao Chen,Yang Yang,Divyavardhan Singh,Hariom Thacker,Hammad Mohammad,Aanchal Maurya,Kishor Upla,Kiran Raja,Wei Zhou,Hongyu Huang,Yujin Cho,Grigory Malivenko,Jiachen Tu,Yaokun Shi,Guoyi Xu,Yaoxin Jiang,Jiajia Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了NTIRE首届可控散景渲染挑战赛的结果，44支队伍注册，8支队伍提交有效方案，通过定量指标和专家用户研究评估，多数参赛者基于Bokehlicious基线方法进行改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推动可控散景渲染领域的发展，通过举办挑战赛吸引研究者关注并评估现有方法的性能边界。

Method: 设立双赛道评估体系：第一赛道采用定量保真度指标，第二赛道通过专家小组进行定性用户研究。参赛方法主要基于Bokehlicious基线改进。

Result: 44支注册队伍中8支提交有效方案，在未见过的肖像和复杂主体图像上评估散景效果。

Conclusion: 首届挑战赛揭示了可控散景渲染的当前水平，并为该领域后续研究提供了基准和方向。

Abstract: This study presents the outcomes of the first Controllable Bokeh Rendering Challenge at NTIRE and highlights the most effective submitted methodologies. In total, 44 participants registered for the competition, of which 8 teams submitted valid solutions after the conclusion of the final test phase. All submissions were evaluated on unseen images, focusing on portraits and intricate subjects with complex and visually appealing bokeh phenomena. In addition to the first track focusing on established quantitative fidelity metrics, we conducted a qualitative user study with a panel of experts for a second track focusing on perceptual assessment. As this was the inaugural challenge on this topic, most of the participants focused on refining and extending the Bokehlicious baseline method.

</details>


### [9] [Characterizing Brazilian Atlantic Forest Restoration Outcomes with Geospatial AlphaEarth Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05547)
*Alice Heiman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究使用AlphaEarth Foundation的卫星嵌入模型分析了巴西大西洋森林1,729个修复点，提出"参考轨迹嵌入"概念，通过余弦相似度评估早期修复成效，发现不同土地利用类型在嵌入空间中形成明显聚类，但信号存在噪声。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大西洋森林仅存12-15%的原生覆盖，大规模监测森林修复至关重要，但传统方法受限于实地报告的不可行性以及NDVI等遥感指数的饱和效应，且修复是渐进过程，难以被快速检测。

Method: 利用AlphaEarth Foundation的卫星嵌入模型分析1,729个修复站点，提出"参考轨迹嵌入"概念，以成熟次生森林为参考，通过余弦相似度定义修复成功度量指标。

Result: 不同土地利用类型在嵌入空间中形成明显聚类，可识别出具有清晰变化向量的站点；但嵌入信号存在噪声，需进一步微调以捕获和预测站点元数据。

Conclusion: 卫星嵌入模型在表征早期修复成效方面具有潜力，但当前嵌入层的细粒度不足，仍需优化以超越土地利用分类的粗粒度信息。

Abstract: The Atlantic Forest in Brazil is a critical biodiversity hotspot, yet less than 12-15% of its original cover remains. Although monitoring forest restoration on a large scale is essential, traditional methods are limited by the impracticality of on-the-ground reporting on such a scale and by the saturation of remote-sensing indices such as NDVI. Furthermore, reforestation is a gradual process as opposed to the rapid spectral changes caused by deforestation. In this study, we examine 1,729 restoration sites in São Paulo, using satellite embeddings from the AlphaEarth Foundation's model to evaluate their effectiveness in characterising early restoration success. We introduce the concept of a 'Reference Trajectory Embedding', defining a metric of restoration success based on cosine similarity to reference sites of mature secondary forest. We observe distinct clusters in embedding space according to different land use and land cover (LULC) types, and we can identify sites with clear change vectors. However, the signal can be noisy, and embeddings may require further fine-tuning to capture and predict site metadata beyond LULC.

</details>


### [10] [A Novel Graph-Regulated Disentangling Mamba Model with Sparse Tokens for Enhanced Tree Species Classification from MODIS Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05549)
*Motasem Alkayid,Zhengsen Xu,Saeid Taleghanidoozdoozan,Yimin Zhu,Megan Greenwood,Quinn Ledingham,Zack Dewis,Mabel Heffring,Naser El-Sheimy,Lincoln Linlin Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GDS-Mamba模型，通过图正则化、解耦Mamba架构和稀疏token方法，有效解决了MODIS时间序列数据中树种分类的难题，在加拿大两省实验中分别达到93.94%和80.19%的分类精度，优于12种现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MODIS时间序列的树种分类面临三大挑战：树种间细微差异、空间-光谱-时间信息的强耦合、大规模拓扑上下文建模困难，现有方法难以有效应对。

Method: (1) 设计小批量图正则化方法显式建模输入图像间的拓扑相关性；(2) 提出解耦Mamba架构分离时空谱耦合信息，提取独立的空间模式、光谱特征和物候时序行为；(3) 设计稀疏token方法自适应学习最优token子集，缓解标准Mamba中的相关性衰减问题。

Result: 在加拿大阿尔伯塔省和萨斯喀彻温省的大规模年度MOD13Q1数据上，总体精度达93.94%（省内）和80.19%（跨省），优于12种最先进分类模型。

Conclusion: GDS-Mamba通过图正则化解耦稀疏Mamba架构，有效提升了MODIS时间序列树种分类的精度和效率，为大规模遥感树种制图提供了新范式。

Abstract: Although tree species classification from Moderate Resolution Imaging Spectroradiometer (MODIS) time series data is critical for supporting various environmental applications, it is a challenging task due to several key difficulties: the subtle signature differences among tree species, strong spatial-spectral-temporal information coupling, and the difficulty of modeling large-scale topological context information. To better address these challenges, this paper presents a novel Graph-regulated Disentangled Sparse Mamba model (GDS-Mamba) for enhanced tree species classification, with the following contributions. (1) First, to improve large-scale context modeling, we design a mini-batch graph-regulated approach that explicitly explores topological correlation effects among input images. (2) Second, to disentangle the high-dimensional spatial-spectral-temporal information coupling for improved feature extraction, we propose a novel disentangling Mamba architecture tailored for capturing independent spatial patterns, spectral signatures, and temporal phenology behaviors in MODIS time series. (3) Third, to improve efficiency and subtle feature learning, we design novel sparse token approaches that adaptively learn the optimum subset of tokens to better address the correlation decay problem that bottlenecks standard Mamba models. Extensive experiments using large-scale annual MOD13Q1 data across two Canadian provinces (i.e., Alberta and Saskatchewan) achieved an overall accuracy of 93.94\% in Alberta and 80.19\% in cross-provincial evaluations, outperforming twelve state-of-the-art classification models.

</details>


### [11] [Text-to-CAD Retrieval: a Strong Baseline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05572)
*Honghu Pan,Zibo Du,Daxiang Liu,Chengliang Liu,Xiaoling Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了文本到CAD检索这一新的跨模态检索任务，并基于Text2CAD数据集构建了基准。作者提出了一种统一框架，通过序列编码器（捕捉构建逻辑）和点编码器（提取几何特征）联合学习多模态CAD嵌入，并引入特征解码器通过交叉注意力机制实现隐式多模态对齐，推理时移除解码器以提升检索效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CAD模型检索依赖文件名或目录，效率、可扩展性和准确性有限，而文本到CAD检索作为关键但未被充分探索的任务，对于工业设计复用至关重要。

Method: 提出统一框架，包含序列编码器（捕捉CAD构建逻辑）、点编码器（提取几何点云特征）和文本编码器（学习文本语义表示）；训练时引入特征解码器，通过交叉注意力利用文本和点特征重建掩码序列特征，实现隐式多模态对齐；推理时移除解码器，使用拼接的序列-点特征进行高效检索。

Result: 在Text2CAD数据集上建立了文本到CAD检索的基准，提出的框架作为强基线方法，为下游CAD生成任务（如检索增强生成）奠定了基础。

Conclusion: 该工作首次正式定义文本到CAD检索任务，提出的多模态对齐框架有效实现了跨模态检索，为工业设计复用和CAD生成范式提供了新方向。

Abstract: Text-based retrieval of Computer-Aided Design (CAD) models is a critical yet underexplored task for the reuse of legacy industrial designs. Existing CAD repositories are typically searched using filenames or directories, which limits the efficiency, scalability, and accuracy of design retrieval. In this paper, we formally introduce text-to-CAD retrieval as a new cross-modal retrieval task, aiming to retrieve semantically relevant CAD models from large-scale databases given natural language queries. Leveraging paired text-CAD annotations from the Text2CAD dataset, we establish a practical benchmark for this task. To achieve text-based retrieval, we propose a unified framework that learns multi-modal CAD embeddings from both procedural sequences and geometric point clouds. Specifically, a sequence encoder captures the construction logic of CAD models, while a point encoder extracts explicit geometric features. A text encoder is used to learn semantic representations of textual queries. During training, we introduce a novel feature decoder that reconstructs masked sequence features via cross-attention with text and point features, encouraging implicit multi-modal alignment. At inference time, we remove this auxiliary decoder to enable efficient retrieval using concatenated sequence-point features. Our framework serves as a strong baseline for text-to-CAD retrieval and lays the foundation for downstream CAD generation paradigms, such as retrieval-augmented generation. The source code will be released.

</details>


### [12] [Leveraging Image Generators to Address Training Data Scarcity: The Gen4Regen Dataset for Forest Regeneration Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05627)
*Gabriel Jeanson,David-Alexandre Duclos,William Larrivée-Hardy,Noé Cochet,Matěj Boxan,Anthony Deschênes,François Pomerleau,Philippe Giguère*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出利用视觉语言模型Nano Banana Pro生成合成图像和像素级语义掩码，解决森林再生树种细粒度语义分割中数据稀缺和类别不平衡问题。结合真实数据与AI生成数据训练，F1分数提升超过15个百分点，少数物种提升达30个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统地面调查和基于无人机的深度学习解释面临专家标注数据极度稀缺和类别不平衡的双重挑战，尤其在复杂异质的森林再生区域，难以实现精细尺度的树种组成映射。

Method: 利用大规模视觉语言模型Nano Banana Pro从提示词生成高保真图像及像素级对齐的语义掩码；构建WilDReF-Q-V2（13977张无标注、50张标注真实图像）和Gen4Regen（2101对合成图像与掩码）数据集；将真实数据与AI生成数据统一训练。

Result: 统一训练相比纯监督基线F1分数提升超15个百分点；部分稀有物种每类F1分数提升达30个百分点；AI生成数据与真实数据高度互补。

Conclusion: 视觉语言模型可作为敏捷数据生成器，有效启动专家标签稀缺的特定AI领域的感知任务。

Abstract: Sustainable forest management relies on precise species composition mapping, yet traditional ground surveys are labour-intensive and geographically constrained. While Uncrewed Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) offer scalable data collection, the transition to deep learning-based interpretation is bottlenecked by the severe scarcity of expert-annotated imagery, particularly in complex, visually heterogeneous regeneration zones. This paper addresses the dual challenges of data scarcity and extreme class imbalance in the semantic segmentation of fine-grained forest regeneration species by providing a scalable framework that reduces reliance on manual photo-interpretation for high-resolution, millimetre-level aerial imagery. Importantly, we leverage the large-scale vision-language Nano Banana Pro model to simultaneously generate high-fidelity images and their corresponding pixel-aligned semantic masks from prompts. We introduce WilDReF-Q-V2, an expansion of a natural forest dataset with 13 977 new unlabelled and 50 labelled real images, as well as the Gen4Regen dataset, featuring 2101 pairs of synthetic images and semantic masks. Our methodology integrates real-world data with AI-generated images, highlighting that AI-generated data is highly complementary to real-world data, with unified training yielding an F1 score improvement of over 15 %pt compared to purely supervised baselines. Furthermore, we demonstrate that even small quantities of prompt-generated data significantly improve performance for underrepresented species, some of which saw per-species F1 score gains of up to 30 %pt. We conclude that vision-language models can serve as agile data generators, effectively bootstrapping perception tasks for niche AI domains where expert labels are scarce or unavailable. Our datasets, source code, and models will be available at https://norlab-ulaval.github.io/gen4regen.

</details>


### [13] [AffectSeek: Agentic Affective Understanding in Long Videos under Vague User Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05640)
*Zhen Zhang,Yuhang Yang,Yunxiang Jiang,Yuhuan Lu,Haifeng Lu,Zheng Lian,Runhao Zeng,Xiping Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出模糊查询驱动的视频情感理解任务（VQAU），构建VQAU-Bench基准数据集，并提出AffectSeek智能体框架，通过多步推理实现长视频中情感时刻的定位、分类与解释。实验表明现有模型在此任务上表现不佳，而AffectSeek提供了有效的解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前情感理解研究主要集中在从图像、音频或预裁剪视频片段中识别情感，这种被动式、以片段为中心的方式无法反映真实场景中用户通过自然语言查询与长视频交互的需求。

Method: 提出AffectSeek智能体框架，将VQAU分解为意图解释、候选定位、片段验证、情感推理和理由生成五个步骤，通过角色专业化推理和跨阶段验证逐步对齐模糊用户意图与长视频证据。

Result: VQAU任务对现有情感识别模型和单步视觉语言模型具有挑战性，而AffectSeek提供了简单有效的框架用于智能体式长视频情感理解。

Conclusion: VQAU是一个具有挑战性的新任务，需要语义-时间-情感的多维对齐能力，AffectSeek框架通过多步推理有效解决了这一问题。

Abstract: Existing affective understanding studies have mainly focused on recognizing emotions from images, audio signals, or pre-cliped video clips, where the affective evidence is already given. This passive and clip-centered setting does not fully reflect real-world scenarios, in which users often interact with long videos and express their needs through natural-language queries. In this paper, we study \textbf{Vague-Query-driven video Affective Understanding (VQAU)}, a new task that requires models to localize affective moments in long videos, predict their emotion categories, and generate evidence-grounded rationales under vague user queries. To support this task, we construct \textbf{VQAU-Bench}, a benchmark that integrates long videos, vague affective queries, temporal clip annotations, emotion labels, and rationale explanations into a unified evaluation framework. VQAU-Bench enables systematic assessment of semantic-temporal-affective alignment, affective moment localization, emotion classification, and rationale generation. To address the multi-step reasoning challenges of VQAU, we further propose \textbf{AffectSeek}, an agentic framework that actively seeks, verifies, and explains affective moments in long videos. AffectSeek decomposes VQAU into intent interpretation, candidate localization, clip verification, emotion reasoning, and rationale generation, and progressively aligns vague user intent with long-video evidence through role-specialized reasoning and cross-stage verification. Experiments show that VQAU remains challenging for existing affective recognition models and single-step vision-language models, while AffectSeek provides a simple yet effective framework for agentic long-video affective understanding.

</details>


### [14] [MUSE: Resolving Manifold Misalignment in Visual Tokenization via Topological Orthogonality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05646)
*Panqi Yang,Haodong Jing,Jiahao Chao,Tingyan Xiang,Li Lin,Yao Hu,Yang Luo,Yongqiang Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MUSE通过拓扑正交性解耦视觉tokenization中的重建与语义优化，将破坏性干扰转化为相互增强，在生成质量（gFID 3.08）和线性探测（85.2% vs 82.5%）上均达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一视觉tokenization面临高保真像素重建（空间等变性）与语义抽象（概念不变性）之间的根本性权衡，现有联合优化导致梯度对抗，形成零和博弈。

Method: 提出MUSE框架，基于拓扑正交性（Topological Orthogonality）将结构作为正交桥，在Transformer内解耦优化：结构梯度精炼注意力拓扑，语义梯度更新特征值，将破坏性干扰转为相互增强。

Result: 生成质量gFID达3.08，线性探测超越其教师模型InternViT-300M（85.2% vs 82.5%），打破了重建与感知之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 结构对齐的重建能够增强语义感知，证明重建与感知并非零和博弈，可通过拓扑正交性实现相互增强。

Abstract: Unified visual tokenization faces a fundamental trade-off between high-fidelity pixel reconstruction (spatial equivariance) and semantic abstraction (conceptual invariance). We attribute this conflict to Manifold Misalignment: naive joint optimization induces opposing gradients, creating a zero-sum game between reconstruction and perception. To address this, we propose MUSE, a framework based on Topological Orthogonality. By treating Structure as an orthogonal bridge, MUSE decouples optimization within Transformers: structural gradients refine attention topology, while semantic gradients update feature values. This turns destructive interference into Mutual Reinforcement. Experiments show that MUSE breaks the trade-off, achieving state-of-the-art generation quality (gFID 3.08) and surpassing its teacher InternViT-300M in linear probing (85.2\% vs. 82.5\%), demonstrating that structurally aligned reconstruction can enhance semantic perception. Code is available at https://github.com/PanqiYang1/MUSE.

</details>


### [15] [Sparse-to-Complete: From Sparse Image Captures to Complete 3D Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05664)
*Yiyang Shen,Yin Yang,Kun Zhou,Tianjia Shao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: S2C-3D是一种新型稀疏视图3D重建框架，仅需6-8张图像即可实现高保真、完整的场景重建，通过场景专用扩散模型、训练自由的视图一致性条件采样和相机轨迹规划三大组件，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有稀疏视图3D重建方法在输入视图极少的情况下，常出现缺失区域、模糊或伪影等问题，难以实现高质量的场景重建。

Method: 提出S2C-3D框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) 对预训练扩散模型进行微调，使其适应特定场景分布，修复高斯渲染结果并消除域差距；(2) 训练自由的视图一致性条件采样过程，量化相邻修复图像间的一致性并将其注入冻结扩散模型的采样过程，生成多视图一致的图像；(3) 相机轨迹规划方案，通过连接新采样相机与最近邻构建路径，保留显著提升可见性的路径，实现完整场景覆盖。

Result: 实验结果表明，S2C-3D在极稀疏输入条件下（6-8张图像）优于现有最先进方法，构建的高质量场景无缺失区域、模糊或其他伪影。

Conclusion: S2C-3D通过场景自适应扩散模型与轨迹规划相结合，有效解决了稀疏视图下的多视图冲突和场景覆盖问题，实现了高保真3D场景重建。

Abstract: We introduce S2C-3D, a novel sparse-view 3D reconstruction framework for high-fidelity and complete scene reconstruction from as few as six to eight images. Our framework features three components: a specialized diffusion model for scene-specific image restoration, a training-free view-consistency conditioned sampling process in the diffusion model for refined Gaussian optimization, and a camera trajectory planning scheme to ensure comprehensive scene coverage. The specialized diffusion model is developed by finetuning a pretrained architecture on the input views and their corresponding degraded counterparts. The adaptation to the scene distribution allows the model to repair Gaussian renderings while effectively eliminating domain gaps. Meanwhile, the trajectory planning scheme optimizes scene coverage by connecting each newly sampled camera to its two nearest neighbors. By iteratively constructing paths and retaining only those that significantly enhance visibility, the scheme establishes a trajectory that covers the entire scene. To address multi-view conflicts, the view-consistency conditioned sampling process quantifies the consistency between neighboring repaired images. This information is injected as a condition into the sampling process of the frozen diffusion model, facilitating the generation of view-consistent images without additional training. Consequently, our approach produces high-fidelity 3D Gaussians that are robust to artifacts. Experimental results demonstrate that S2C-3D outperforms state-of-the-art methods, constructing high-quality scenes that are free from missing regions, blurring, or other artifacts with very sparse inputs. The source code and data are available at https://gapszju.github.io/S2C-3D.

</details>


### [16] [MotionGRPO: Overcoming Low Intra-Group Diversity in GRPO-Based Egocentric Motion Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05680)
*Nanjie Yao,Junlong Ren,Wenhao Shen,Hao Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MotionGRPO框架，利用强化学习后训练（GRPO）优化扩散模型，通过混合奖励机制（全局感知+局部关节约束）和噪声注入策略，解决头戴设备信号中全身3D人体运动重建的局部关节误差问题，达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散的人体运动恢复方法依赖全局分布匹配，导致局部关节重建误差较大，缺乏细粒度引导机制。

Method: 将扩散采样建模为马尔可夫决策过程，采用GRPO优化；设计混合奖励机制（学习型条件感知模型+显式关节约束）；提出噪声注入策略解决组内样本多样性不足导致的梯度消失问题。

Result: 在全身3D运动恢复任务上达到最先进性能，视觉逼真度优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 强化学习后训练+噪声注入能有效提升扩散模型在局部关节精度和全局视觉合理性上的表现，为头戴设备信号的人体运动重建提供了新范式。

Abstract: This paper studies full-body 3D human motion recovery from head-mounted device signals. Existing diffusion-based methods often rely on global distribution matching, leading to local joint reconstruction errors. We propose MotionGRPO, a novel framework leveraging reinforcement learning post-training to inject fine-grained guidance into the diffusion process. Technically, we model diffusion sampling as a Markov decision process optimized via Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO). To this end, we introduce a hybrid reward mechanism that combines a learned conditioned perceptual model for global visual plausibility and explicit constraints for local joint precision. Our key technical insight is that policy optimization in diffusion-based recovery suffers from vanishing gradients due to limited intra-group sample diversity. To address this, we further introduce a noise-injection strategy that explicitly increases sample variance and stabilizes learning. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MotionGRPO achieves state-of-the-art performance with superior visual fidelity

</details>


### [17] [CFE-PPAR: Compression-friendly encryption for privacy-preserving action recognition leveraging video transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05692)
*Haiwei Lin,Shoko Imaizumi,Hitoshi Kiya*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出首个压缩友好的隐私保护动作识别加密方法CFE-PPAR，通过密钥对齐的视频Transformer实现加密视频直接识别，在UCF101和HMDB51数据集上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于加密的PPAR方法在视频压缩后会出现识别性能和视觉质量的灾难性下降，即不具压缩友好性，需要解决此问题。

Method: 提出CFE-PPAR方法，使用密钥对视频进行加密，同时用相同密钥转换视频Transformer的参数，使加密后的视频可直接被Transformer识别，无需解密。

Result: 在UCF101和HMDB51数据集上，分别在Motion-JPEG和H.264压缩下验证了CFE-PPAR优于先前方法。

Conclusion: CFE-PPAR实现了压缩友好的隐私保护动作识别，解决了加密方法在视频压缩场景下的性能退化问题。

Abstract: Privacy-preserving action recognition (PPAR) enables machines to understand human activities in videos without revealing sensitive visual content. Among the various strategies for PPAR, encryption-based methods achieve strong privacy protection while maintaining high recognition performance. However, these methods lead to a catastrophic decrease in recognition performance and visual quality when the encrypted videos are compressed. That is, the previous methods are not compression-friendly. To address these issues, in this paper, we propose the first compression-friendly encryption method for PPAR, called CFE-PPAR. In CFE-PPAR, videos encrypted with secret keys can be directly recognized by a video transformer, which uses parameters transformed by the same keys as those used for video encryption. In experiments, it is verified that CFE-PPAR outperforms previous methods on the UCF101 and HMDB51 datasets under Motion-JPEG and H.264 compression.

</details>


### [18] [Adaptive Physical-Facial Representation Fusion via Subject-Invariant Cross-Modal Prompt Tuning for Video-Based Emotion Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05694)
*Xiwen Luo,Jia Li,Rencheng Song,Yu Liu,Juan Cheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向视频情绪识别的主题不变跨模态提示微调框架，通过rPPG时频表示生成提示调制冻结ViT的面部特征，并引入解耦共享-特定适配器(DSSA)分离主题共享与特定成分，在MAHNOB-HCI和DEAP数据集上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有融合面部和rPPG特征的多模态方法存在两个问题：(1) 融合策略常破坏预训练的面部表征；(2) 缺乏抑制个体间差异的显式机制，导致对未见过的个体泛化能力受限。

Method: (1) 将rPPG波形转换为噪声鲁棒的时频表示(TFRs)；(2) 从TFRs生成模态互补提示，调制冻结Vision Transformer(ViT)中的面部token，保持预训练骨干的泛化能力；(3) 在每层ViT中引入解耦共享-特定适配器(DSSA)，显式分离主题共享与主题特定成分。

Result: 在MAHNOB-HCI和DEAP基准数据集上，所提方法在识别准确率和泛化能力上均持续优于强基线方法。

Conclusion: 该方法通过跨模态提示调优和显式解耦个体差异，有效提升了视频情绪识别的准确性和跨个体泛化能力。

Abstract: Emotion recognition from facial videos enables non-contact inference of human emotional states. Although facial expressions are widely used cues, they cannot fully reflect intrinsic affective states. Remote photoplethysmography (rPPG) provides complementary physiological information, but it is highly susceptible to noise and inter-subject variability, limiting generalization to unseen individuals. Existing multimodal methods combine facial and rPPG features, yet their fusion strategies often disrupt pretrained facial representations and lack explicit mechanisms to suppress subject-specific variations. To address these issues, we propose a subject-invariant cross-modal prompt-tuning framework for video-based emotion recognition. Specifically, rPPG waveforms are transformed into noise-robust time-frequency representations (TFRs), from which modality-complementary prompts are generated to modulate facial tokens within a frozen Vision Transformer (ViT). This design enables effective cross-modal interaction while preserving the generalizable facial representations learned by the pretrained backbone. In addition, we introduce a decoupled shared-specific adapter (DSSA) into each ViT layer to explicitly separate subject-shared and subject-specific components, thereby improving cross-subject generalization. Experiments on the MAHNOB-HCI and DEAP benchmarks demonstrate that the proposed method consistently outperforms strong baselines in both recognition accuracy and generalization ability, highlighting its effectiveness for video-based emotion recognition.

</details>


### [19] [Closing the Loop: Unified 3D Scene Generation and Immersive Interaction via LLM-RL Coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05711)
*Anh H. Vo,Sungyo Lee,Phil-Joong Kim,Soo-Mi Choi,Yong-Guk Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一框架，将语言驱动的3D场景生成与沉浸式用户交互闭环整合，通过LLM构建场景表示、强化学习优化空间布局，并在VR环境中实现人机交互闭环反馈，在ALFRED基准上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将场景生成与用户交互视为分离过程，限制了交互式多媒体系统的适应性和沉浸感，需要弥合生成与交互之间的鸿沟。

Method: 1) 利用LLM根据自然语言指令构建结构化场景表示；2) 通过强化学习在几何和语义约束下优化空间布局；3) 在VR环境中部署场景，通过人机交互循环提供连续反馈以对齐生成内容与人类感知。

Result: 在ALFRED基准上取得SOTA任务型场景生成性能；用户研究和定性结果表明在沉浸感、交互质量和任务效率方面均有一致提升。

Conclusion: 生成与交互的闭环整合对于下一代交互式多媒体系统至关重要，能够实现更响应、自适应和逼真的多媒体体验。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have significantly improved language-driven 3D content generation, but most existing approaches still treat scene generation and user interaction as separate processes, limiting the adaptability and immersive potential of interactive multimedia systems. This paper presents a unified framework that closes the loop between language-driven 3D scene generation and immersive user interaction. Given natural language instructions, the system first constructs structured scene representations using LLMs, and then optimizes spatial layouts via reinforcement learning under geometric and semantic constraints. The generated environments are deployed in a virtual reality setting to facilitate HRI-in-the-loop, where user interactions provide continuous feedback to align generated content with human perception and usability. By tightly coupling generation and interaction, the proposed framework enables more responsive, adaptive, and realistic multimedia experiences. Experiments on the ALFRED benchmark demonstrate state-of-the-art performance in task-based scene generation. Furthermore, qualitative results and user studies show consistent improvements in immersion, interaction quality, and task efficiency, highlighting the importance of closed-loop integration of generation and interaction for next-generation multimedia systems. Our project page can be found at https://proj-showcase.github.io/h3ds/.

</details>


### [20] [EgoEMG: A Multimodal Egocentric Dataset with Bilateral EMG and Vision for Hand Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05712)
*Ziheng Xi,Jiayi Yu,Yitao Wang,Yanbo Duan,Jianjiang Feng,Jie Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EgoEMG是一个多模态双手机器人数据集，包含sEMG、IMU、RGB视频、RGB-D视频和动作捕捉数据，覆盖41名参与者、60种手势类别、超过10小时记录，并提供了EMG-to-pose、vision-to-pose和EMG+vision融合三个基准任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究缺乏同时同步采集sEMG与第一视角视觉的双模态数据集，而两者在手部感知上具有互补性：EMG能捕捉精细手指运动且不受遮挡和光照影响，视觉则提供全局手部配置。

Method: 构建了包含双侧腕带EMG（16通道/2kHz）、IMU（120Hz）、第一视角广角RGB视频、外部RGB-D视频及动作捕捉（含腕关节角度）的多模态数据集；设计了跨手势、跨用户和联合泛化三个评估轴线的基准任务。

Result: 提供了EMGFormer用于EMG-to-pose、ResNet/ViT用于vision-to-pose的基线结果，并提出了一种残差融合架构，在轻量级视觉基线基础上实现了性能提升。

Conclusion: EgoEMG及其基准任务为未来基于EMG与视觉的多模态手部姿态估计研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Surface electromyography (sEMG) records muscle activity during hand movement and can be decoded to recover detailed hand articulation. EMG and egocentric vision are complementary for hand sensing: EMG captures fine-grained finger articulation even under occlusion and poor lighting, while vision provides global hand configuration. However, no existing dataset synchronizes both modalities. We present EgoEMG, a multimodal egocentric dataset for bimanual hand pose estimation. EgoEMG includes bilateral wristband EMG with 16 total channels (8 per wrist) sampled at 2 kHz, 120 Hz IMU, egocentric wide-angle RGB video, external RGB-D video, and mocap-derived hand motion with wrist articulation angles. The dataset covers 41 participants performing 60 gesture classes, including 30 single-hand gestures and 30 bimanual gestures, totaling more than 10 hours of recording. We also introduce a benchmark with three tasks -- EMG-to-pose, vision-to-pose, and EMG+vision fusion -- under a shared joint-angle prediction target and common generalization split axes (cross-gesture, cross-user, and combined). As baselines, we evaluate EMGFormer for EMG-to-pose and generic ResNet/ViT backbones for vision-to-pose. We further study a residual fusion architecture that improves over matched lightweight vision-only baselines. Together, EgoEMG and its benchmark establish a foundation for future research on multimodal hand pose estimation with EMG and vision.

</details>


### [21] [TriRelVLA: Triadic Relational Structure for Generalizable Embodied Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05714)
*Hanyu Zhou,Chuanhao Ma,Gim Hee Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TriRelVLA提出三元关系视觉-语言-动作框架，通过构建物体-手-任务三元关系表示和关系瓶颈，实现跨场景、跨物体、跨任务的可泛化具身操作，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型在训练见过的任务上表现良好，但难以泛化到未见场景和物体。关键限制在于隐式视觉表示纠缠了物体外观、背景和场景布局，导致策略对视觉变化敏感。先前工作通过结构化中间表示提升了迁移性，但这些表示主要捕捉场景语义而非与动作相关的关系，导致动作预测仍依赖外观统计信息。

Method: 1) 从多模态输入构建显式的物体-手-任务三元关系表示作为关系原语；2) 构建任务引导的关系图，通过任务引导的交叉注意力形成节点，并用关系感知图Transformer建模节点间交互；3) 进行关系条件化的动作生成，将关系结构压缩到瓶颈空间并投影到大语言模型中进行动作预测。同时引入真实世界机器人数据集进行微调。

Result: 在微调任务上表现强劲，在跨场景、跨物体和跨任务泛化方面均取得显著提升。

Conclusion: 三元关系瓶颈减少了对外观统计信息的依赖，实现了跨场景、物体和任务组合的迁移。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models perform well on training-seen robotic tasks but struggle to generalize to unseen scenes and objects. A key limitation lies in their implicit visual representations, which entangle object appearance, background, and scene layout. This makes policies sensitive to visual variations. Prior work improves transferability through structured intermediate representations that objectify visual content. However, these representations mainly capture scene semantics instead of action-relevant relations. As a result, action prediction remains tied to appearance statistics. We observe that manipulation actions depend on the object-hand-task relational structure, which governs interactions among task requirements, robot states, and object properties. Based on this observation, we propose TriRelVLA, a triadic relational VLA framework for generalizable embodied manipulation. Our approach consists of three components: 1) We construct explicit object-hand-task triadic representations from multimodal inputs as relational primitives. 2) We build a task-grounded relational graph. Task-guided cross-attention forms nodes, and a relation-aware graph transformer models interactions among them. 3) We perform relation-conditioned action generation. The relational structure is compressed into a bottleneck space and projected into the LLM for action prediction. This triadic relational bottleneck reduces reliance on appearance statistics and enables transfer across scenes, objects, and task compositions. We further introduce a real-world robotic dataset for fine-tuning. Experiments show strong performance on fine-tuned tasks and clear gains in cross-scene, cross-object, and cross-task generalization.

</details>


### [22] [Ray-Aware Pointer Memory with Adaptive Updates for Streaming 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05749)
*Feifei Li,Qi Song,Chi Zhang,Rui Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向流式3D重建的射线感知指针内存机制，通过显式建模空间位置和视角方向，采用保留-替换策略替代传统融合式内存压缩，并利用射线差异检测回环以触发位姿优化，实现了长程稳定且抗漂移的在线3D重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前馈式流式3D重建框架依赖外观相似性进行内存更新，导致视角变化时出现观测冗余积累和几何不稳定问题。

Method: 1) 提出射线感知指针内存（Ray-aware Pointer Memory），每个指针存储3D位置、关联射线方向和特征嵌入，联合建模空间邻近性和视角一致性。2) 设计自适应指针更新策略，采用保留-替换机制替代传统融合式压缩，选择性保留信息性指针并丢弃冗余指针。3) 利用空间距离和射线方向差异统一区分局部冗余、新观测和回环重访，检测到回环候选时触发位姿优化以保持全局几何一致性。

Result: 大量实验表明，所提方法在长程重建稳定性和相机位姿精度上显著提升，同时保持高效的流式推理性能。

Conclusion: 该方法为从图像流中进行可扩展、抗漂移的在线3D重建提供了一个有原则的框架。

Abstract: Dense 3D reconstruction from continuous image streams requires both accurate geometric aggregation and stable long-term memory management. Recent feed-forward reconstruction frameworks integrate observations through persistent memory representations, yet most rely primarily on appearance-based similarity when updating memory. Such appearance-driven integration often leads to redundant accumulation of observations and unstable geometry when viewpoint changes occur. In this work, we propose a ray-aware pointer memory for streaming 3D reconstruction that explicitly models both spatial location and viewing direction within a unified memory representation. Each memory pointer stores its 3D position, associated ray direction, and feature embedding, allowing the system to reason jointly about geometric proximity and viewpoint consistency. Based on this representation, we introduce an adaptive pointer update strategy that replaces traditional fusion-based memory compression with a retain-or-replace mechanism. Instead of averaging nearby observations, the system selectively retains informative pointers while discarding redundant ones, preserving distinctive geometric structures while maintaining bounded memory growth. Furthermore, the joint reasoning over spatial distance and ray-direction discrepancy enables the system to distinguish between local redundancy, novel observations, and potential loop revisits in a unified manner. When loop candidates are detected, pose refinement is triggered to enforce global geometric consistency across the reconstruction. Extensive experiments demonstrate that the proposed ray-aware memory design significantly improves long-term reconstruction stability and camera pose accuracy while maintaining efficient streaming inference. Our approach provides a principled framework for scalable and drift-resistant online 3D reconstruction from image streams.

</details>


### [23] [Jointly Learning Structured Representations and Stabilized Affinity for Human Motion Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05753)
*Xianghan Meng,Zhiyuan Huang,Zhengyu Tong,Chun-Guang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TDSC方法，通过联合学习时序一致的结构化表示和稳定亲和度，解决人类运动分割中原始帧级特征违反子空间并集假设的问题，在五个基准数据集上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于子空间聚类的人类运动分割方法假设高维时序特征符合子空间并集分布，但真实世界视频的原始帧级特征常违反该假设，导致分割性能不佳。

Method: 提出TDSC方法：1) 通过带有编码率最大化正则化的自表达模型交替学习结构化表示和自表达系数，避免表示坍塌并符合UoS分布；2) 引入时序约束促进相邻帧划分到同一组；3) 设计时序动量平均机制稳定亲和度演化；4) 采用重参数化策略实现高效优化。

Result: 在五个基准HMS数据集上，使用传统HoG特征和现代深度特征（CLIP、DINOv2）均验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: TDSC通过联合学习时序一致的结构化表示并稳定亲和度，有效解决了帧级特征不符合UoS假设的问题，实现了准确鲁棒的人类运动分割。

Abstract: Human Motion Segmentation (HMS), which aims to partition a video into non-overlapping segments corresponding to different human motions, has recently attracted increasing research attention. Existing HMS approaches are predominantly based on subspace clustering, which are grounded on the assumption that the distribution of high-dimensional temporal features well aligns with a Union-of-Subspaces (UoS). For videos in the real world, however, the raw frame-level features often violate the UoS assumption and yield unsatisfactory segmentation performance. To address this issue, we propose an efficient and effective approach for HMS, named Temporal Deep Self-expressive subspace Clustering (TDSC), which jointly learns temporally consistent structured representations and stabilized affinity for accurate and robust HMS. Specifically, in TDSC, we alternately learn structured representations of the input frame features and self-expressive coefficients via a properly regularized self-expressive model, in which a coding-rate maximization regularizer is incorporated to avoid representation collapse and conform the learned representations to span a desired UoS distribution, and meanwhile, temporal constraints are incorporated to promote temporally adjacent frames to be partitioned into the same groups. Moreover, we develop a temporal momentum averaging mechanism to stabilize affinity evolution and design a reparameterization strategy to enable efficient optimization. We conduct extensive experiments on five benchmark HMS datasets using both conventional (HoG) and up-to-date deep features (i.e., CLIP, DINOv2) to validate the effectiveness of our approach.

</details>


### [24] [iTRIALSPACE: Programmable Virtual Lesion Trials for Controlled Evaluation of Lung CT Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05761)
*Fakrul Islam Tushar,Umme Hafsa Momy,Joseph Y. Lo,Geoffrey D. Rubin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: iTRIALSPACE是一个可编程评估框架，通过四阶段管道（结节分析、试验规范、掩膜插入、ControlNet条件CT合成）构建受控虚拟病变试验，以解决静态基准无法分离影响因素的局限性。在55,469样本虚拟病变研究中验证，合成数据质量达真实基线水平，性能排名与临床数据高度相关（ρ=0.93），并揭示了固定分布基准无法发现的捷径行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准静态基准因混杂病灶大小、肺叶分布、解剖结构及采集环境等因素，难以确定模型精度的结构性驱动因素，需要一种可控、可审计的评估基础设施。

Method: 四阶段管道：1) 基于7个公开CT源的13,140个结节构建54属性统一结节档案数据集；2) 显式试验规范定义；3) 解剖感知掩膜插入；4) ControlNet条件CT合成。框架包含13种试验模式。

Result: 合成数据FID达到真实基线；合成与临床性能排名高度相关(ρ=0.93, p<10⁻¹⁵)；发现快捷尺寸预测崩溃（肺叶均衡采样下）以及宿主-供体方差比达8.9x和3.3x。

Conclusion: iTRIALSPACE可作为超越静态回顾性基准的可审计评估基础设施，支持受控、可证伪的模型测试。

Abstract: We introduce iTRIALSPACE, a programmable evaluation framework for controlled assessment of lung CT models. Standard benchmarks are static retrospective collections that entangle lesion size, lobe prevalence, anatomy, and acquisition context, making it difficult to determine what structurally drives model accuracy. iTRIALSPACE addresses this limitation by composing real clinical CTs and lesion profiles into controlled virtual lesion trials through a four-stage pipeline: multidataset nodule profiling, explicit trial specification, anatomy-aware mask insertion, and ControlNet-conditioned CT synthesis. The framework is built on a unified 54-attribute nodule-profile dataset spanning 13,140 annotated nodules from seven public CT sources and instantiated as 13 trial modes. We evaluate iTRIALSPACE in a 55,469-sample Virtual Lesion Study spanning three medical VLMs, four spatialguidance conditions, and three clinical tasks. Across all 13 modes, the synthetic substrate remains within the real-to-real FID baseline, and synthetic performance rankings transfer strongly to real clinical data ($ρ$ = 0.93, p < 10$^{-15}$). Controlled trial modes expose findings unavailable to fixed-distribution benchmarks, including shortcut-driven size prediction collapse under lobe-equalized sampling and hostto-donor variance ratios of 8.9x and 3.3x in twin-cross analysis. These results position iTRIALSPACE as an auditable evaluation infrastructure for controlled, falsifiable testing beyond static retrospective benchmarks.

</details>


### [25] [X-OmniClaw Technical Report: A Unified Mobile Agent for Multimodal Understanding and Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05765)
*Xiaoming Ren,Ru Zhen,Chao Li,Yang Song,Qiuxia Hou,Yanhao Zhang,Peng Liu,Qi Qi,Quanlong Zheng,Qi Wu,Zhenyi Liao,Binqiang Pan,Haobo Ji,Haonan Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 X-OmniClaw，一个面向 Android 生态的统一移动端智能体，通过 Omni 感知、Omni 记忆和 Omni 动作三大模块实现多模态理解与交互，有效提升复杂移动任务的交互效率和可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受 OpenClaw 启发，移动端个人智能体需能处理复杂且直观的交互，但目前缺乏统一的移动端多模态理解与交互架构。

Method: 提出统一架构，包含三个核心模块：(1) Omni 感知——统一多模态输入管道，整合 UI 状态、视觉上下文和语音输入，通过时序对齐模块将原始数据转化为结构化多模态意图表示；(2) Omni 记忆——结合运行时工作记忆（任务连续性）与长期个人记忆（从本地数据蒸馏），通过多模态记忆优化增强个性化智能；(3) Omni 动作——采用混合定位策略，融合结构 XML 元数据与视觉感知实现鲁棒交互，并通过行为克隆与轨迹回放将用户导航捕获为可复用技能。

Result: 在多种场景下的演示表明，X-OmniClaw 有效提升了交互效率和任务可靠性。

Conclusion: X-OmniClaw 为下一代移动端原生个人助理提供了实用的架构蓝图。

Abstract: Inspired by the development of OpenClaw, there is a growing demand for mobile-based personal agents capable of handling complex and intuitive interactions. In this technical report, we introduce X-OmniClaw, a unified mobile agent designed for multimodal understanding and interaction in the Android ecosystem. This unified architecture of perception, memory, and action enables the agent to handle complex mobile tasks with high contextual awareness. Specifically, Omni Perception provides a unified multimodal ingress pipeline that integrates UI states, real-world visual contexts, and speech inputs, leveraging a temporal alignment module to decompose raw data into structured multimodal intent representations. Omni Memory leverages multimodal memory optimization to enhance personalized intelligence by integrating runtime working memory for task continuity with long-term personal memory distilled from local data, enabling highly context-aware and personalized interactions. Finally, Omni Action employs a hybrid grounding strategy that combines structural XML metadata with visual perception for robust interaction. Through Behavior Cloning and Trajectory Replay, the system captures user navigation as reusable skills, enabling precise direct-access execution. Demonstrations across diverse scenarios show that X-OmniClaw effectively enhances interaction efficiency and task reliability, providing a practical architectural blueprint for the next generation of mobile-native personal assistants.

</details>


### [26] [Steering Visual Generation in Unified Multimodal Models with Understanding Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05781)
*Zeyu Liu,Zanlin Ni,Yang Yue,Cheng Da,Huan Yang,Di Zhang,Kun Gai,Gao Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出理解导向的后训练框架UNO，通过将理解任务作为生成表征的监督信号，在图像生成和编辑任务中验证了理解能力可以促进生成性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统一多模态模型在理解和生成任务上采用解耦设计，虽然各自表现优异，但削弱了两者之间的相互增强潜力，这种协同作用在实证上尚不确定。

Method: 提出UNO框架，将理解任务不仅视为独立任务，还作为引导生成表征的直接监督信号。通过引入语义抽象（字幕生成）和结构细节（视觉回归）两种目标，实现从理解到生成的有效梯度传播。

Result: 在图像生成和图像编辑任务上的大量实验表明，理解能力可以有效地催化生成性能的提升。

Conclusion: 理解与生成之间可以通过适当的训练框架实现协同增强，理解可以作为生成任务的有效催化剂。

Abstract: Unified multimodal models are envisioned to bridge the gap between understanding and generation. Yet, to achieve competitive performance, state-of-the-art models adopt largely decoupled understanding and generation components. This design, while effective for individual tasks, weakens the connection required for mutual enhancement, leaving the potential synergy empirically uncertain. We propose to explicitly restore this synergy by introducing Understanding-Oriented Post-Training (UNO), a lightweight framework that treats understanding not only as a distinct task, but also a direct supervisory signal to steer generative representations. By incorporating objectives that encode semantic abstraction (captioning) and structural details (visual regression), we enable effective gradient flow from understanding to generation. Extensive experiments on image generation and editing demonstrate that understanding can serve as an effective catalyst for generation.

</details>


### [27] [Na-IRSTD: Enhancing Infrared Small Target Detection via Native-Resolution Feature Selection and Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05804)
*Qian Xu,Chi Zhang,Qiming Zhang,Xi Li,Haojuan Yuan,Mingjin Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Na-IRSTD提出了一种原生分辨率特征提取与融合框架用于红外小目标检测，通过保留原生分辨率细节和高效令牌缩减选择策略，在四个基准数据集上达到了最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有红外小目标检测方法通常采用传统下采样策略，导致小目标的细节信息丢失，定位性能不佳。

Method: 提出Na-IRSTD框架，包含原生分辨率特征提取与融合模块，以及令牌缩减与选择策略，在保持高精度的同时降低计算负担。

Result: 在四个公开基准数据集上取得了最先进的性能，令牌缩减选择策略展现出鲁棒性和有效性。

Conclusion: Na-IRSTD通过原生分辨率特征保留和高效令牌选择，有效解决了红外小目标检测中细节丢失与计算效率的权衡问题。

Abstract: Infrared small target detection (IRSTD) faces the inherent challenge of precisely localizing dim targets amid complex background clutter. While progress has been made, existing methods usually follow conventional strategies to downsample features and discard small targets' details, resulting in suboptimal performance. In this paper, we present Na-IRSTD, a native-resolution feature extraction and fusion framework for IRSTD. This framework elegantly incorporates native-resolution features to preserve subtle target cues, overcoming the resolution limitations of existing infrared approaches and significantly improving the model's ability to localize small targets. We also introduce an effective token reduction and selection strategy, which selects target patches with high accuracy and confidence, boosting the low-level details of the feature while effectively reducing native-resolution patch tokens compared to dense processing, thereby avoiding imposing an unbearable computational burden. Extensive experiments demonstrate the robustness and effectiveness of our token reduction and selection strategy across multiple public datasets. Ultimately, our Na-IRSTD model achieves state-of-the-art performance on four benchmarks.

</details>


### [28] [CXR-ContraBench: Benchmarking Negated-Option Attraction in Medical VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05810)
*Zhengru Fang,Yanan Ma,Yu Guo,Senkang Hu,Yixian Zhang,Hangcheng Cao,Wenbo Ding,Yuguang Fang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文揭示了医学视觉语言模型在胸部X光片分析中存在的“否定选项吸引”现象——即便影像中存在病变，模型仍倾向于选择“无病变”选项。作者构建了CXR-ContraBench基准测试集，发现MedGemma和Qwen2.5-VL在直接存在性探测任务中准确率仅约30%，且超过62%的样本选择了否定选项。链式思维提示只能部分缓解问题，而提出的QCCV-Neg方法无需重训即可将准确率提升至95%以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学视觉语言模型在回答选择题时，当影像中存在病变但选项为“无病变”的否定形式时，模型会系统性选择否定选项，产生与影像证据相矛盾的临床陈述。这种“极性反转”错误在传统准确率评估中被掩盖，具有重大的临床安全风险，亟需系统性研究和修复。

Method: (1) 构建CXR-ContraBench基准，涵盖内部ReXVQA、外部OpenI和CheXpert数据集，包含存在性问题和缺失性问题两类； (2) 系统性评估MedGemma和Qwen2.5-VL在直接存在性探测和CheXpert大规模协议上的表现； (3) 测试链式思维提示的效果； (4) 提出QCCV-Neg（条件一致性验证器），通过确定性修复方式纠正极性混淆错误。

Result: (1) MedGemma和Qwen2.5-VL在直接存在性探测上准确率仅31.49%和30.21%； (2) 在13.5万条CheXpert训练协议中，两者选择否定选项的比例超过62%； (3) 链式思维可部分减少存在性反转但会加剧缺失性矛盾； (4) QCCV-Neg将两模型在直接存在性探测上的准确率提升至96.60%和95.32%。

Conclusion: 标准准确率指标会掩盖医学视觉语言模型在推理时存在的临床意义极性反转错误。否定选项吸引是一个系统性、持久性的失败模式，需专门设计的方法（如QCCV-Neg）进行修复，而非简单的提示工程。

Abstract: When a chest X-ray shows consolidation but the question asks which finding is present, a medical vision-language model may answer "No consolidation." This is more than an incorrect choice: it is a polarity reversal that emits a clinical statement contradicting the image. We study this failure as negated-option attraction, where a model is drawn to a negated answer option even when it conflicts with both the visual evidence and the question. We introduce CXR-ContraBench (Chest X-Ray Contradiction Benchmark), a diagnostic benchmark spanning internal ReXVQA slices and external OpenI and CheXpert protocols. The benchmark centers on present-finding questions, where selecting "No X" despite visible X creates the main clinical risk, and uses absent-finding questions as secondary tests of whether models copy negated wording. Across CheXpert protocols, the failure is substantial and persistent. On a strict direct presence probe, MedGemma and Qwen2.5-VL reach only 31.49% and 30.21% accuracy, respectively; on a matched 135,754-record CheXpert training-split protocol, both models select negated options on over 62% of presence questions. Chain-of-thought prompting reduces some presence-side reversals but does not eliminate them and can amplify absence-side contradictions. Finally, QCCV-Neg (Question-Conditioned Consistency Verifier for Negation) deterministically repairs the measured polarity-confused subset without retraining, raising MedGemma and Qwen2.5-VL to 96.60% and 95.32% accuracy on the direct presence probe. These results show that standard accuracy can hide a clinically meaningful inference-time polarity failure. Source code and benchmark construction scripts are available at https://github.com/fangzr/cxr-contrabench-code.

</details>


### [29] [ChartZero: Synthetic Priors Enable Zero Shot Chart Data Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05820)
*Md Touhidul Islam,Yasir Mahmud,Sujan Kumar Saha,Mark Tehranipoor,Farimah Farahmandi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ChartZero提出了一种基于合成数据先验的零样本图表数据提取框架，通过全局正交实例损失解决曲线碎片化问题，并利用视觉语言模型引导的图例匹配策略替代传统空间规则，在无需真实数据标注的情况下实现了泛化性能的显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有线图数据提取方法受限于极端风格多样性、真实世界标注数据稀缺、曲线碎片化与细粒度视觉细节丢失、基于空间规则的图例匹配脆弱性，以及缺乏端到端整体评估指标等根本性问题。

Method: ChartZero框架包含三个核心创新：(1) 仅在纯合成简单数学函数数据集上训练，完全规避真实标注瓶颈；(2) 提出全局正交实例（GOI）损失函数以克服曲线碎片化；(3) 采用开放词汇的视觉语言模型（VLM）引导的图例匹配策略替代脆弱的空间启发式规则；并配套设计了面向完整端到端重建的新指标与基准。

Result: 实验表明ChartZero在无需真实世界监督的条件下，显著推进了通用化图表数据数字化能力，证明了合成先验在零样本图表提取中的有效性。

Conclusion: ChartZero成功破解了真实标注瓶颈，通过合成数据训练、GOI损失和VLM引导的图例匹配三大技术，实现了鲁棒的零样本线图数据提取，并建立了更合理的端到端评估体系。

Abstract: Automated data extraction from line charts remains fundamentally bottlenecked by extreme stylistic diversity and a severe scarcity of comprehensively annotated, real-world datasets. Current end-to-end pipelines depend heavily on costly manual annotations, crippling their ability to generalize across arbitrary aesthetics and grid layouts. Furthermore, existing models suffer from two critical failure modes during reconstruction. First, extracting thin, intersecting curves frequently causes structural fragmentation and the erasure of fine visual details, as standard architectures struggle against complex backgrounds. Second, semantic association is notoriously error-prone; current pipelines rely on rigid spatial heuristics that easily break down against the unpredictable legend placements of in-the-wild charts. Finally, measuring true progress is hindered by evaluation protocols that assess isolated sub-tasks rather than holistic, end-to-end data reconstruction. To address these foundational issues, we introduce ChartZero, a parsing framework that leverages synthetic priors to enable robust zero-shot chart data extraction. By training exclusively on a purely synthetic dataset of simple mathematical functions, our model completely bypasses the real-world annotation bottleneck. We overcome curve fragmentation via a novel Global Orthogonal Instance (GOI) loss, and replace brittle spatial rules with an open-vocabulary, Vision-Language Model (VLM)-guided legend matching strategy. Accompanied by a new metric and benchmark specifically designed for full end-to-end reconstruction, our evaluations demonstrate that ChartZero significantly advances generalized plot digitization without requiring real-world supervision. Code and dataset will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [30] [Unifying Scientific Communication: Fine-Grained Correspondence Across Scientific Media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05831)
*Megha Mariam K. M,Vineeth N. Balasubramanian,C. V. Jawahar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了多模态会议数据集（MCD），这是首个整合同一研究工作的研究论文、演示视频、解说视频和幻灯片的数据集，并系统评估了嵌入模型和视觉语言模型在跨格式细粒度对齐任务上的表现，揭示了当前方法的优势与局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学知识传播日益多模态化（文本、视觉、语音），但不同格式材料之间缺乏结构化连接，难以追踪概念、视觉和解释之间的对应关系，限制了研究内容的统一探索与分析。

Method: 构建多模态会议数据集（MCD），整合同一研究工作的论文、演示视频、解说视频和幻灯片；评估基于嵌入的模型和视觉语言模型在跨格式细粒度对应发现任务上的性能。

Result: 视觉语言模型表现鲁棒但在细粒度对齐上存在困难；基于嵌入的模型能较好捕捉文本-视觉对应，但方程和符号内容在嵌入空间中形成独立聚类。

Conclusion: 当前方法在跨模态科学理解上各有优劣，指出了未来研究的关键方向，并开源了数据集以保证可复现性。

Abstract: The communication of scientific knowledge has become increasingly multimodal, spanning text, visuals, and speech through materials such as research papers, slides, and recorded presentations. These different representations collectively convey a study's reasoning, results, and insights, offering complementary perspectives that enrich understanding. However, despite their shared purpose, such materials are rarely connected in a structured way. The absence of explicit links across formats makes it difficult to trace how concepts, visuals, and explanations correspond, limiting unified exploration and analysis of research content. To address this gap, we introduce the Multimodal Conference Dataset (MCD), the first benchmark that integrates research papers, presentation videos, explanatory videos, and slides from the same works. We evaluate a range of embedding-based and vision-language models to assess their ability to discover fine-grained cross-format correspondences, establishing the first systematic benchmark for this task. Our results show that vision-language models are robust but struggle with fine-grained alignment, while embedding-based models capture text-visual correspondences well but equations and symbolic content form distinct clusters in the embedding space. These findings highlight both the strengths and limitations of current approaches and point to key directions for future research in multimodal scientific understanding. To ensure reproducibility, we release the resources for MCD at https://github.com/meghamariamkm2002/MCD

</details>


### [31] [VideoRouter: Query-Adaptive Dual Routing for Efficient Long-Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05848)
*Kuanwei Lin,Wenhao Zhang,Ge Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VideoRouter提出了一种查询感知的双路由器框架（语义路由器+图像路由器），用于长视频中预算化的视觉证据分配，在保持甚至提升性能的同时，最高可减少67.9%的视觉token。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频中的视觉token序列过长，导致推理时内存和延迟显著增加。现有压缩方法要么是弱查询感知的，要么对所有帧应用固定压缩策略，在视觉证据时间分布不均匀时效果不佳。

Method: 基于InternVL构建查询感知的双路由器框架：语义路由器预测主导分配策略（广泛时间覆盖 vs 自适应高分辨率保留），图像路由器利用早期LLM层对帧相关性评分，从而对不相关帧进行激进压缩、对关键证据帧保留细节。同时构建了两个训练数据集：Video-QTR-10K（分配策略监督）和Video-FLR-200K（帧相关性监督）。

Result: 在VideoMME、MLVU和LongVideoBench上，VideoRouter在相同或更低预算下持续优于InternVL基线，最高实现67.9%的token缩减。

Conclusion: 查询感知的、预算化的证据分配策略能够有效解决长视频中视觉token瓶颈问题，通过区分关键帧与非关键帧并动态调整压缩策略，在显著降低计算开销的同时保持甚至提升视频理解性能。

Abstract: Video large multimodal models increasingly face a scalability bottleneck: long videos produce excessively long visual-token sequences, which sharply increase memory and latency during inference. While existing compression methods are effective in specific settings, most are either weakly query-aware or apply a fixed compression policy across frames, proving suboptimal when visual evidence is unevenly distributed over time. To address this, we present VideoRouter, a query-adaptive dual-router framework built on InternVL for budgeted evidence allocation. The Semantic Router predicts the dominant allocation policy, choosing between broad temporal coverage and adaptive high-resolution preservation, while the Image Router uses early LLM layers to score frame relevance. This enables aggressive compression on less relevant frames while preserving detail on critical evidence frames. To train both routers, we build Video-QTR-10K for allocation-policy supervision and Video-FLR-200K for frame-relevance supervision. Experiments on VideoMME, MLVU, and LongVideoBench show that VideoRouter consistently improves over the InternVL baseline under comparable or lower budgets, achieving up to a 67.9% token reduction.

</details>


### [32] [Align3D-AD: Cross-Modal Feature Alignment and Dual-Prompt Learning for Zero-shot 3D Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05850)
*Letian Bai,Xuanming Cao,Juan Du,Chengyu Tao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Align3D-AD 提出一种统一的两阶段零样本3D异常检测框架，通过跨模态特征对齐和模态感知提示学习，利用辅助类别的RGB模态作为跨模态指导，解决了现有方法中预训练编码器与投影表示之间的域差异问题，在多个数据集上取得了最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零样本3D异常检测方法主要依赖将3D观测投影为多视图表示，这些表示主要捕获几何线索而非逼真的视觉语义，使用在RGB数据上预训练的视觉编码器处理时存在显著的域差异，导致检测性能受限。

Method: 提出Align3D-AD两阶段框架：(1) 跨模态特征对齐范式，将渲染特征映射到RGB语义空间，并引入语义一致性重加权策略细化对齐；(2) 模态感知提示学习框架，为RGB对齐特征和渲染特征分配独立提示，通过双提示对比对齐增强判别能力。

Result: 在MVTec3D-AD、Eyecandies和Real3D-AD数据集上，该方法的性能优于现有零样本方法。

Conclusion: Align3D-AD在一下一余和跨数据集设置下均展现出优异的泛化能力和鲁棒性，有效解决了零样本3D异常检测中的域差异问题。

Abstract: Zero-shot 3D anomaly detection aims to identify anomalies without access to training data from target categories. However, existing methods mainly rely on projecting 3D observations into multi-view representations that primarily capture geometric cues rather than realistic visual semantics and process them with vision encoders pretrained on RGB data, leading to a significant domain gap between the encoder and the projected representations. To address this issue, we propose Align3D-AD, a unified two-stage framework that leverages the RGB modality from auxiliary categories as cross-modal guidance for zero-shot 3D anomaly detection. First, we introduce a cross-modal feature alignment paradigm that maps rendering features into the RGB semantic space. Unlike prior works that implicitly rely on pretrained encoders, our method enables direct semantic transfer from RGB observations. A semantic consistency reweighting strategy is further introduced to refine feature alignment by reweighting local regions according to holistic semantic consistency. Second, we propose a modality-aware prompt learning framework with dual-prompt contrastive alignment. By assigning independent prompts to RGB-aligned and rendering features, our method captures complementary semantics across modalities, while the contrastive alignment further enhances prompt representations to improve discriminability. Extensive experiments on MVTec3D-AD, Eyecandies, and Real3D-AD demonstrate that Align3D-AD consistently outperforms existing zero-shot methods under both one-vs-rest and cross-dataset settings, highlighting its generalization capability and robustness. Code and the dataset will be made available once our paper is accepted.

</details>


### [33] [InkDiffuser: High-Fidelity One-shot Chinese Calligraphy via Differentiable Morphological Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05865)
*Kunchong Shi,Jing Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: InkDiffuser 是一个基于扩散模型的中文书法单样本生成框架，通过高频增强机制和可微墨迹结构损失（DIS）实现了高保真的笔画渲染和逼真的墨迹形态，仅需一个参考字符即可生成优于现有方法的书法字体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有中文书法生成方法存在笔画渲染质量差、墨迹形态不真实的问题，导致生成结果视觉保真度和艺术流畅性有限。

Method: 提出 InkDiffuser 扩散生成框架，包含两个核心贡献：(1) 高频增强机制，利用样本中的高频信息提取轮廓细节以提升字体结构准确性；(2) 可微墨迹结构损失（DIS），将可微分形态学操作集成到扩散过程中，显式正则化墨迹形态并实现笔画轮廓的细粒度优化。

Result: 在多种书法风格和复杂字符上的大量实验表明，InkDiffuser 仅需单个参考字形即可生成具有逼真墨迹渲染效果的优质书法字体，在结构一致性、细节保真度和视觉真实性方面均优于现有的少样本字体生成方法。

Conclusion: InkDiffuser 通过高频增强和可微形态学约束有效解决了书法生成中笔画渲染和墨迹形态不真实的问题，为单样本中文书法合成提供了高性能解决方案。

Abstract: Current Chinese calligraphy generation methods suffer from poor stroke rendering and unrealistic ink morphology, resulting in outputs with limited visual fidelity and artistic fluidity. To address this problem, we propose \textbf{InkDiffuser}, a diffusion-based generative framework for one-shot Chinese calligraphy synthesis. To guarantee high-fidelity rendering, we introduce two core contributions: a high-frequency enhancement mechanism and a Differentiable Ink Structure (DIS) loss that explicitly regularizes ink morphology. Inspired by the observation that high-frequency information in individual samples typically carries contour details, we enhance content extraction by explicitly fusing high-frequency representations for more accurate font structure. Furthermore, we propose a differentiable ink structure loss that integrates differentiable morphological operations into the diffusion process. By allowing the model to learn an explicit decomposition of ink-trace structures, DIS facilitates fine-grained refinement of stroke contours and delivers significantly improved visual realism in the generated calligraphy. Extensive experiments on various calligraphic styles and complex characters demonstrate that InkDiffuser can generate superior calligraphy fonts with realistic ink rendering effects from only a single reference glyph and outperform existing few-shot font generation approaches in structural consistency, detail fidelity, and visual authenticity. The code is available at the following address: https://github.com/JingVIPLab/InkDiffuser.

</details>


### [34] [Training-Free Dense Hand Contact Estimation with Multi-Modal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05886)
*Daniel Sungho Jung,Kyoung Mu Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ContactPrompt，一种无需训练、零样本的密集手部接触估计方法，利用多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）通过手部分割、逐部分顶点网格表示和多阶段结构化接触推理，在无需训练的情况下超越先前监督方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有密集手部接触估计方法需要大规模标注数据，且MLLMs虽在视觉语义理解上表现优异，但难以编码3D手部几何并捕捉细粒度顶点级接触。作者旨在利用MLLMs的推理能力实现无需训练的密集手部接触估计。

Method: 提出ContactPrompt，包含三个关键组件：1）详细的手部分割和逐部分顶点网格表示，将3D手部几何编码为结构化局部信息；2）多阶段结构化接触推理，通过部分条件逐步连接全局语义和细粒度几何；3）利用MLLMs的推理能力进行零样本密集接触预测。

Result: 该方法在无需任何训练的情况下，超越了先前在大规模密集接触数据集上训练的监督方法。

Conclusion: ContactPrompt有效利用MLLMs的推理能力实现了精准的密集手部接触估计，证明了零样本方法在该任务上的可行性。

Abstract: Dense hand contact estimation requires both high-level semantic understanding and fine-grained geometric reasoning of human interaction to accurately localize contact regions. Recently, multi-modal large language models (MLLMs) have demonstrated strong capabilities in understanding visual semantics, enabled by vision-language priors learned from large-scale data. However, leveraging MLLMs for dense hand contact estimation remains underexplored. There are two major challenges in applying MLLMs to dense hand contact estimation. First, encoding explicit 3D hand geometry is difficult, as MLLMs primarily operate on vision and language modalities. Second, capturing fine-grained vertex-level contact remains challenging, as MLLMs tend to focus on high-level semantics rather than detailed geometric reasoning. To address these challenges, we propose ContactPrompt, a training-free and zero-shot approach for dense hand contact estimation using MLLMs. To effectively encode 3D hand geometry, we introduce a detailed hand-part segmentation and a part-wise vertex-grid representation that provides structured, localized geometric information. To enable accurate and efficient dense contact prediction, we develop a multi-stage structured contact reasoning with part conditioning, progressively bridging global semantics and fine-grained geometry. Therefore, our method effectively leverages the reasoning capabilities of MLLMs while enabling precise dense hand contact estimation. Surprisingly, the proposed approach outperforms previous supervised methods trained on large-scale dense contact datasets without requiring any training. The codes will be released.

</details>


### [35] [DBMSolver: A Training-free Diffusion Bridge Sampler for High-Quality Image-to-Image Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05889)
*Sankarshana Venugopal,Mohammad Mostafavi,Jonghyun Choi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DBMSolver，一种无需训练的扩散桥模型采样器，通过指数积分器利用SDE/ODE的半线性结构，将所需函数评估次数减少5倍，同时提升生成质量（如DIODE数据集上FID下降53%），在多种图像到图像任务中实现了新的效率-质量平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散桥模型（DBMs）在图像到图像翻译中生成质量高，但采样速度慢，需要数十次函数评估，限制了实际应用。

Method: 提出DBMSolver，一种无需训练的采样器，利用扩散桥模型底层SDE和ODE的半线性结构，通过指数积分器推导出一阶和二阶高效数值解。

Result: 在图像修复、风格迁移、语义到图像生成等任务中（分辨率最高256x256），DBMSolver将NFEs减少多达5倍，同时提升质量（DIODE数据集上FID降低53%），实现了新的效率-质量最优平衡。

Conclusion: DBMSolver通过指数积分器充分利用DBM的半线性结构，显著加速采样过程且无需额外训练，推动了扩散桥模型在实际场景中的可用性。

Abstract: Diffusion-based image-to-image (I2I) translation excels in high-fidelity generation but suffers from slow sampling in state-of-the-art Diffusion Bridge Models (DBMs), often requiring dozens of function evaluations (NFEs). We introduce DBMSolver, a training-free sampler that exploits the semi-linear structure of DBM's underlying SDE and ODE via exponential integrators, yielding highly-efficient 1st- and 2nd-order solutions. This reduces NFEs by up to 5x while boosting quality (e.g., FID drops 53% on DIODE at 20 NFEs vs. 2nd-order baseline). Experiments on inpainting, stylization, and semantics-to-image tasks across resolutions up to 256x256 show DBMSolver sets new SOTA efficiency-quality tradeoffs, enabling real-world applicability. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/snumprlab/dbmsolver.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: CNN-only模型在多脚本训练下改进有限且不稳定，而CRNN模型在多脚本训练中表现更好，尤其在数据最受限的场景下；跨语言性能提升主要与序列级建模相关，仅共享CNN编码器学习的视觉表征（如相似字符形状）不足以带来有效迁移。

Abstract: Handwritten Text Recognition (HTR) for Arabic-script languages benefits from cross-language joint training under low-resource conditions, particularly when using CRNN-based models that combine convolutional encoders with sequence modeling. However, it remains unclear whether these improvements are better explained by shared visual representations or sequence-level dependencies. In this work, we conduct a controlled architectural study of line-level Arabic-script HTR, comparing CNN-only models with CTC decoding and CRNN models under identical single-script and multi-script training regimes. Experiments are performed on Arabic (KHATT), Urdu (NUST-UHWR), and Persian (PHTD) datasets under low-resource settings (K in {100, 500, 1000}). Our results show a clear divergence in transfer behavior: while CNN-only models exhibit limited or unstable improvements, CRNN models achieve better performance under multi-script training, particularly in the most data-constrained regimes. Focusing on transfer improvements (delta CER) rather than absolute performance, we find that cross-language improvements are associated with sequence-level modeling, while sharing visual representations learned by the CNN encoder, corresponding to similarities in character shapes across scripts, alone appears to be insufficient. This finding suggests that contextual modeling plays an important role in enabling effective transfer in low-resource scenarios, and that similar behavior may extend to other low-resource language settings.

</details>


### [37] [Architecture-agnostic Lipschitz-constant Bayesian header and its application to resolve semantically proximal classification errors with vision transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05908)
*Frederik Schäfer,Luis Mandl,Lars Kälber,Tim Ricken*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种架构无关的Lipschitz约束贝叶斯头（LipB-ViT），通过谱归一化约束变分权重的均值和log方差，实现了在结构化标签噪声下的校准不确定性估计和噪声鲁棒性，在语义错误标签识别上召回率超过0.93，相比SOTA方法提升7%以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标签噪声是监督深度学习的瓶颈，特别是结构化而非随机的语义邻近分类错误，现有鲁棒训练方法在此时往往失效。

Method: 1) 提出架构无关的Lipschitz-constant贝叶斯头，集成到特征提取器（如ViT）中形成LipB-ViT；2) 对变分权重的均值和log方差施加谱归一化，构成bi-Lipschitz约束；3) 提出联合捕捉不确定性和置信度的新度量；4) 设计自适应算术平均融合方案，结合特征空间邻近性和预测不确定性来检测错误标签。

Result: 在15%语义错误标签下，召回率超过0.93，相比基于k近邻的SOTA方法提升超过7%；在推理时的结构化（对抗性）和非结构化噪声下均表现出鲁棒性。

Conclusion: LipB-ViT提供即插即用兼容性，超参数跨域一致，虽因蒙特卡洛采样增加计算成本，但在高标注可靠性变化的高风险应用中具有显著优势。

Abstract: Label noise remains a critical bottleneck for the generalization of supervised deep learning models, particularly when errors are structured rather than random. Standard robust training methods often fail in the presence of such semantically proximal classification errors.
